Otherwise use one format directly.

4. Seed, mirror, and one pass-finalizer

The cache host pass keeps color in `VulkanRenderTarget::kColorDrawLayout` before and after the pass ([lines 2215-2216](/C:/Users/leanerdesigner/Documents/New%20project%208/xenia-thor/src/xenia/gpu/vulkan/vulkan_render_target_cache.cc:2215)). Record all copies after a render pass has ended—never inside one.

Before `CmdVkBeginRenderPass` for the replacement framebuffer:

1. Barrier LLE color: `kColorDrawLayout -> kColorDrawLayout`,
   `COLOR_ATTACHMENT_WRITE -> TRANSFER_READ`,
   `COLOR_ATTACHMENT_OUTPUT -> TRANSFER`.
2. Barrier native color: tracked layout (`UNDEFINED` first use, otherwise `kColorDrawLayout`) to `TRANSFER_DST_OPTIMAL`,
   destination access `TRANSFER_WRITE`.
3. `CmdVkCopyImage(lle_color, kColorDrawLayout, native, TRANSFER_DST_OPTIMAL, 1280x720)`.
4. Barrier both back:
   - LLE: `TRANSFER_SRC_OPTIMAL` is unnecessary; it remained color-draw layout, so make its post-copy barrier from transfer read to color-attachment read/write without a layout change.
   - Native: `TRANSFER_DST_OPTIMAL -> kColorDrawLayout`, `TRANSFER_WRITE -> COLOR_ATTACHMENT_READ|WRITE`, `TRANSFER -> COLOR_ATTACHMENT_OUTPUT`.
5. Begin the original LLE render pass using the cloned framebuffer.

More simply, leave the LLE source in `kColorDrawLayout` throughout seed; Vulkan permits a same-layout image barrier.

After the swapped pass ends:

1. Native: `kColorDrawLayout -> TRANSFER_SRC_OPTIMAL`,
   `COLOR_ATTACHMENT_WRITE -> TRANSFER_READ`.
2. LLE: `kColorDrawLayout -> TRANSFER_DST_OPTIMAL`,
   source masks covering prior transfer-read/color-attachment access, destination `TRANSFER_WRITE`.
3. `CmdVkCopyImage(native, TRANSFER_SRC_OPTIMAL, lle_color, TRANSFER_DST_OPTIMAL, 1280x720)`.
4. Restore both to `kColorDrawLayout`, with transfer read/write visible to `COLOR_ATTACHMENT_OUTPUT` and `COLOR_ATTACHMENT_READ|WRITE`.

Use ignored queue-family indices and a single color subresource, mip 0, layer 0. The existing `ResolveMsaa` barriers are a good command-buffer style reference ([bd_native_renderer.cc:85](/C:/Users/leanerdesigner/Documents/New%20project%208/xenia-thor/src/xenia/gpu/vulkan/bd_native_renderer.cc:85)); this Level‑4 gate must reject MSAA rather than resolve it.

Create one helper such as `EndCurrentRenderPassAndFinalizeBdColorMirror()`. Call it immediately after each current guest-pass `CmdVkEndRenderPass`:

- the RT-change raw end at [vulkan_command_processor.cc:4600](/C:/Users/leanerdesigner/Documents/New%20project%208/xenia-thor/src/xenia/gpu/vulkan/vulkan_command_processor.cc:4600)
- the normal `EndRenderPass` raw end at [line 4949](/C:/Users/leanerdesigner/Documents/New%20project%208/xenia-thor/src/xenia/gpu/vulkan/vulkan_command_processor.cc:4949)

Run the mirror before `current_render_pass_` / `current_framebuffer_` are cleared. This covers `IssueCopy`, `IssueSwap`, and submission teardown because they route through `EndRenderPass`.

5. First Thor test: round-trip only

1. Start from a clean process with all existing native renderer/present/drop flags off.
2. Set only `gpu_bd_native_color_lifetime_hle=4`.
3. Keep the mirror target private—not in `BdNativeRenderer::surfaces_`—so the Level‑2 present redirect at [IssueSwap:3412](/C:/Users/leanerdesigner/Documents/New%20project%208/xenia-thor/src/xenia/gpu/vulkan/vulkan_command_processor.cc:3412) cannot find it.
4. For every 9567 producer pass: split if necessary, seed LLE→native, render with LLE depth, mirror native→LLE, then execute the ordinary LLE `IssueCopy` and ordinary LLE present.
5. Log `seed`, `mirror`, source RT fingerprint, pass key, and post-resolve destination. Accept only logs where the post-resolve destination alternates `1CA1C000` / `1CDB4000`.
6. Capture identical stable-field baseline and Level‑4 screenshots, plus logcat, APK/build hash, settings, and route. The screenshots must match; any mismatch means stop—no present redirect or dropped resolve follows.

Risk order:

1. Render-pass/view-format/sample mismatch — likely Turnip device loss; prevent with the strict `0b11`, 1x, exact-pass gate.
2. Wrong layout/access ordering around copies — visible corruption or validation failure; keep every copy outside passes and restore LLE color layout exactly.
3. Producer identification/pass splitting — may add a harmless extra LLE store/load, but cannot change output because mirror restores LLE before resolve.
4. Depth — lowest risk if it is untouched: reuse the exact LLE depth view and original pass unchanged.
